Resilience ^EPI1Vabi

The barrier to entry to draft partially functional code including entire proof of concept applications is lowering.

From this a new development methodology is emerging where initial versions of code is generated by AI, at times by inexperienced developers.

The developer starts with code they didn't write and assumes it has hidden flaws.

Then finding flaws and adding features drives further development.

This creates a loop of generating uncertainty and unfamiliarity that must be managed. ^LAH942QD

Fuzzy Buddy ^ZpIjlvMz

Fuzzing is a trusted technique historically used for finding exploitable gaps in input handling.

It has applicability to finding non-security flaws.

Unlike automated tests it can find code paths not considered by developers or the shape of the system.

This makes it effective at verifying code that is unfamiliar

In addition, where automated tests scale with development time, fuzzing does so with clock time. 

The tradeoff being automated tests make specific claims like "opens model" and fuzzing makes generic claims like "doesn't crash".

This  generalization is what allows us to test cases we did not think to test. 

Unfortunately integrating fuzzing into your full stack development workflow is a project in itself. 

It currently requires additional expertise to...
      adapt fuzzing tools to project 
      interpret its results

It also doesn't lend itself to checkbox CI/CD, as it's ideally a continuous running process.
 ^ujoyiRbc

  From analyzing this arises these core concerns and how we can add value

Monitoring  ^3gxwBtDj

A new coding practice is emerging whereby initial versions of code
are written by AI and further development is driven by humans.
Finding and addressing defects and inadequacies drives productivity under this workflow. 

Unfortunately this workflow tends towards the accumulation of uncertainty and defects.

Consider the workflow as an alternating sequence of AI and human changes.
Each AI change introduces uncertainty in the behavior of the system, wherein could hide defects and inadequacies.
Each human change hopes to address these shortcomings, but only can as far as the developer can penetrate the uncertainty of the system.
Assuming occasionally there is missed behavior, uncertainty and shortcomings accumulate over time.

Fortunately, advances in browser automation, sophistication in fuzzing, and theoretical frameworks such as Security Chaos Engineering and Residuality Theory. ^V6KZy6Hd
